ELSE NULL    END  END  WHEN false THEN CASE c.relkind    WHEN 'r' THEN 'TABLE'    WHEN 'i' THEN 'INDEX'    WHEN 'S' THEN 'SEQUENCE'    WHEN 'v' THEN 'VIEW'    ELSE NULL  END  ELSE NULL  END  AS TABLE_TYPE, d.description AS REMARKS  FROM pg_catalog.pg_namespace n, pg_catalog.pg_class c  LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_description d ON (c.oid = d.objoid AND d.objsubid = 0)  LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_class dc ON (d.classoid=dc.oid AND dc.relname='pg_class') LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_namespace dn ON (dn.oid=dc.relnamespace AND dn.nspname='pg_catalog')  WHERE c.relnamespace = n.oid
